# MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS requirements

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001` | **Connector pack contract types and golden digest vectors (rollup).** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Entry: served `ConnectorPack.Import` and `AgentComponent.*`. The Rust serde types in `crates/medulla-types/src/connector_pack/` and `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs` are the only source of the pack wire contract. Medulla computes framed SHA-256 digests (`digest.rs::pack_digest`) and checks them against golden vectors in `crates/medulla-types/tests/fixtures/connector_pack_digest_vectors.json`. The hand-written Python client reads the same vectors. Children `.1` to `.9` and `.100` deliver it. | Root test `tests/test_connector_pack_contract.py::test_pack_index_entry_contract` (L4) passes through `.1` to `.5`. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.1` | **Pack index and entry types.** Code: `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/index.rs` (`ConnectorPackIndex`, entry). Rule: every index and entry field round-trips through MessagePack. An unknown entry kind and a malformed index field refuse. Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs`. Depends on: none. Caller chain: served `ConnectorPack.Import` → `handle_connector_pack` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:43) → `serve_import` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146) → `preflight_import` → `validate_import_identity` → `validate_and_commit` → `validate_index` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:20). Shape: the closed types `ConnectorPackIndex` (crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/index.rs:120), `PackEntry` (crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/index.rs:80) and `PackEntryKind` (crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/index.rs:17) stay serde types; the round trip uses `medulla_storage::encode_bounded`, the encoder `validate_import_identity` already uses for the index size bound; add the test to the `tests` module of `index.rs`. | Test: `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/index.rs::tests::index_entry_round_trip_and_refusals` (L1, planned) round-trips every field and refuses an unknown entry kind and a malformed field. Verified by the root test. |

Rule: every section and annotation field round-trips. Invalid section bounds and malformed typed annotations refuse. Caller: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/annotations.rs::check_annotations`. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `validate_and_commit` → `prepare` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:95) → `validate_index` → `IndexValidator::validate_entry` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:154) → `validate_sections` and `validate_annotations` → `check_annotations` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/annotations.rs:38). Shape: `PackAnnotations` (crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/annotations.rs:66) and `PackSection` in `index.rs` are the only types; section bounds reuse `section_bytes` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:895), which uses checked arithmetic. | Test: `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/annotations.rs::tests::new_fields_roundtrip` (L1) plus `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import.rs::tests::section_ranges_are_checked_without_wrapping` (L2) for invalid section bounds. |

Rule: operation and result shapes stay stable. A missing operation or an incompatible result shape fails. Caller: `Method::ConnectorPack` and `Method::AgentComponent`. Depends on: none. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.4` | **Framed digest vectors.** Code: `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/digest.rs` (`pack_digest`, `annotations_digest`). Rule: every sample of the vector file `crates/medulla-types/tests/fixtures/connector_pack_digest_vectors.json` matches byte for byte. A changed framed input or expected digest fails. Digests are SHA-256 with 64 lowercase hex characters. The Python client test reads the same file. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `validate_import_identity` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:332) calls `pack_digest` (crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/digest.rs:318) over the received index and refuses a differing `pack_digest` with `PACK_DIGEST_MISMATCH`; `annotations_digest` (crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/digest.rs:166) feeds `entry_digest`. Shape: the framing helper `list_digest` and the domain constants in `digest.rs` are the only digest code; the vector file is `crates/medulla-types/tests/fixtures/connector_pack_digest_vectors.json`, read by the Rust test and `tests/test_connector_pack_client.py`. | Test: `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/digest.rs::tests::framed_digest_vectors_match` (L1). |

Rule: its models accept and emit the Rust wire shapes and the golden digest bytes. Shape: the models and the digest function in `medulla/connector_pack.py` mirror the Rust serde types and are checked against `crates/medulla-types/tests/fixtures/connector_pack_digest_vectors.json`; add the field-level mutation checks beside the existing vector test in `tests/test_connector_pack_client.py`. | Test: `tests/test_connector_pack_client.py::test_pack_digest_matches_the_rust_golden_vector` (L4) compares the client with the Rust vectors. The missing, extra and changed field cases are `tests/test_connector_pack_contract.py::test_python_client_matches_pack_vectors` (L4, planned). |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.6` | **Versioned prompt graph identity.** Code: extend `SystemPrompt` facts and add `PromptBlueprintIdentity` in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`. `PromptBlueprintIdentity { tenant_id: String, component_id: String, entry_revision: u64, schema_revision: u32, body_sha256: String, provenance: ComponentProvenance }` (`ComponentProvenance` is the existing provenance type of `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`; `body_sha256` is 64 lowercase hex characters; `schema_revision` is 1, the only supported version). The `SystemPrompt` facts gain two pins, `body_sha256: Option<String>` and `schema_revision: Option<u32>` (both `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]`, both `Some` or both `None`); a blueprint prompt must carry both. The identity is derived, not stored: `PromptBlueprintIdentity::derive(tenant_id, component_id, entry_revision, &facts, &provenance)` runs after publish assigns the entry revision, and the publish result returns it. Unsupported versions, invalid ids, a pin present without its pair and a blueprint prompt missing either pin refuse with `PROMPT_IDENTITY_INVALID`. Changing a schema or body pin changes the identity. Caller: `handle_agent_component` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/agent.rs`) → publish. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `Method::AgentComponent` publish → `handle_agent_component` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/agent.rs:99) → `component_publish` → `AgentLibraryStore::publish_component` (crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/agent_component/write.rs:39). Shape: extend the `SystemPrompt` variant of `AgentComponentFacts` (crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/facts.rs:359) and add `PromptBlueprintIdentity` beside it; validation joins `AgentComponentDraft::validate` (crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs:644). Config keys: none. | Test: `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs::tests::prompt_blueprint_identity` (L1) round-trips all fields and checks pin changes. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.7` | **Prompt payload validation.** Code: `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/facts.rs::validate_prompt_blueprint(value: &Value, limits: &PromptLimits) -> Result<(), RefusalCode>`, with `PromptLimits { max_blueprint_bytes: u32 }` declared beside it; the caller reads the config key and passes it, so the lower crate reads no config. The size is `serde_json::to_vec(value).len()`. The blueprint profile is the bounded JSON of plan.md with core fields and preserved extension values. Limits: config key `prompt.max_blueprint_bytes` in the server config, default 262144, range [1024, 4194304], unit bytes. Refusals: `PROMPT_SCHEMA_INVALID` covers a malformed field and an unsupported schema rule; the caller's parse step maps a duplicate key or a nonfinite number to the same code before the function runs. `PROMPT_OUTPUT_TOO_LARGE` covers an exceeded limit. Caller: publish and render paths. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.6. Caller chain: `component_publish` in `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/agent.rs` and the render path of R002.9 call `validate_prompt_blueprint`. Shape: add the function to `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/facts.rs` next to the `SystemPrompt` facts (crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/facts.rs:359); the handler parses with `parse_bounded_json` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/json.rs:9`, made `pub(crate)` in `medulla-h-admin`), which rejects duplicate keys and nonfinite numbers, then calls the function; the byte limit is read from the server config key `prompt.max_blueprint_bytes` and passed in `PromptLimits`. | Test: `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/facts.rs::tests::prompt_blueprint_schema_refusals` (L1) covers a missing task, wrong types, a nonfinite number and an oversized object. It also keeps nested extension values. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.8` | **Deterministic runtime rendering.** Code: new `crates/medulla-compute/src/assemble/prompt.rs::render_prompt(revision: &ValidatedPrompt, vars: &BTreeMap<String, Value>, policy: &RenderPolicy) -> Result<RenderedPrompt, RefusalCode>`. The engine renders; the harness applies the bytes. It resolves only declared variables and serializes the typed object under the versioned rendering policy. `RenderedPrompt` holds bytes and the SHA-256 `render_digest`. Config key `prompt.max_render_bytes`, default 1048576, range [1024, 16777216], unit bytes. Refusals: `PROMPT_VARIABLE_MISSING`, `PROMPT_VARIABLE_UNKNOWN`, `PROMPT_PIN_UNRESOLVED`, `PROMPT_OUTPUT_TOO_LARGE`. Caller: R002.9 route. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.7. `ValidatedPrompt` (new, `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component/facts.rs`) wraps a `Value` that passed `validate_prompt_blueprint` and carries its `PromptBlueprintIdentity` (R001.6); only `validate_prompt_blueprint` constructs it. `RenderPolicy { max_render_bytes: u32, rendering_version: u32 }` (rendering version 1) is declared in `prompt.rs`. Caller chain: `handle_agent_component` Render arm (R002.9) → `render_prompt`. Shape: new file `crates/medulla-compute/src/assemble/prompt.rs`, registered in `crates/medulla-compute/src/assemble.rs`; `medulla-compute` is a leaf crate, so `medulla-h-admin` adds it as a dependency in its `Cargo.toml` and no handler crate calls a sibling. The pin framing is one pure function `frame_pins` in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`, shared with `verify_receipt` (R002.13), so neither crate calls the other. Digest helper: `Digest256::sha256` from `medulla_types::contract`; config key `prompt.max_render_bytes` is read by the handler and passed in `RenderPolicy`. | Test: `crates/medulla-compute/src/assemble/prompt.rs::tests::prompt_render_vectors` (L2) shows equal inputs give equal bytes. A changed variable changes the digest. Invalid input yields no result. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.9` | **Prompt operation wire types.** Code: add `Render`, `VerifyRender` and blueprint envelopes to `AgentComponentOp` in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`. Both are operations of the existing `Method::AgentComponent`, not new `Method` variants, so no new descriptor is added: the existing `AgentComponent` descriptor row in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/domains/storage.rs` covers them, and `AgentComponentOp::is_mutation` returns false and `AgentComponentOp::authz_action` returns `agent:component-read` for each. `Render { request: AgentComponentRenderRequest }` carries `{ tenant_id, component_id, revision: Option<u64>, variables: BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value> }` (at most 64 variables, names 1 to 128 characters). Its result is `{ bytes (serde_bytes), render_digest, entry_revision: u64, schema_revision: u32 }`. `VerifyRender { request: AgentComponentVerifyRenderRequest }` carries the render request fields plus `render_digest` and returns `{ matches: bool }`. `AgentComponentOp::validate` covers the new arms. Add the matching models to the hand-written Python client. Unsupported operation or schema versions refuse. Caller: `Method::AgentComponent`. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `Method::AgentComponent` → `handle_agent_component` → new Render and VerifyRender arms. Shape: add the variants to `AgentComponentOp` (crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs:1143) with request and result structs in the same file, the `is_mutation`, `authz_action`, `validate` and `component_op_tenant_id` arms for both ops, and the matching models in `medulla/client.py`; the guard is the existing `crates/medulla-capabilities/tests/contract_registry.rs`, which needs no change because the `Method` variant set is unchanged. | Test: `tests/test_prompt_blueprint_contract.py::test_prompt_wire_round_trip` (L4) encodes and decodes every request, result and typed refusal against Rust round-trip vectors. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R002` | **Engine-owned body storage with guarded refcounts and reconciliation (rollup).** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Entry: served `ConnectorPack.Import` and `AgentComponent.Content`. Medulla stores component bodies in content-addressed storage keyed by SHA-256 (`crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/engine_bodies.rs`). Guarded holder rows count liveness (`src/server/persistence/connector_pack/`). `AgentComponent.Content` serves verified bytes (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/component_content.rs`). A reconciler (`reconcile.rs`) reclaims unheld bodies after the grace window. Children `.1` to `.13` deliver it. | Root tests are the `storage_acceptance` cases under `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/storage_acceptance.rs` (L3). |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R002.1` | **Body staging.** Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` or `AgentComponent.Content` → `crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/engine_bodies.rs`. Rule: Staged bytes use their verified SHA-256 address. A digest mismatch refuses with `BODY_DIGEST_MISMATCH`. Staging alone exposes no component. Evidence: the holder rows and the body digest. Writes: holder rows through the admitted owner write. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `run_import` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import.rs:66) → `put_engine_bodies` (crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/store/native/engine.rs:8) in `crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/engine_bodies.rs`; `AgentComponent.Content` → `read_engine_body` (crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/engine_bodies.rs:205). Shape: the digest check is the `BODY_DIGEST_MISMATCH` branch of `read_engine_body`; the case goes in `storage_acceptance.rs` beside `multi_component_import_emits_one_durable_event_per_component`. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/storage_acceptance.rs::body_staging` (L3, planned) runs through the served import. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R002.2` | **Unchanged holder transition.** Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` or `AgentComponent.Content` → `src/server/persistence/connector_pack/`. Rule: An unchanged import keeps exact holder and refcount values. A repeated identical import never changes a holder. Evidence: the holder rows and the body digest. Writes: holder rows through the admitted owner write. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `run_import` → `AgentLibraryStore::commit_connector_pack` (crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack/commit.rs:58) → `pack_effects` (holder rows). Shape: holder rows `pack:<tenant>:<sha256>` are written only by `commit.rs`; an unchanged member yields no effect in `PlanCollector::add_member` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:317). | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/admin.rs::holder_refcounts_transition_across_unchanged_revised_and_withdrawn_entries` (L3) runs through the served import. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R002.3` | **Revised holder transition.** Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` or `AgentComponent.Content` → `src/server/persistence/connector_pack/`. Rule: A revised component binds its new body and keeps the holders of historical revisions. A revision never orphans a body held by history. Evidence: the holder rows and the body digest. Writes: holder rows through the admitted owner write. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `run_import` → `commit_connector_pack` → `pack_effects`. Shape: `PlanCollector::resolve_component` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:260) adds the new body holder and keeps prior revisions' holders; the commit side is `crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack/commit.rs`. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/admin.rs::holder_refcounts_transition_across_unchanged_revised_and_withdrawn_entries` (L3) runs through the served import. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R002.4` | **Withdrawn holder transition.** Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` or `AgentComponent.Content` → `src/server/persistence/connector_pack/`. Rule: A withdrawn component keeps body access through a valid historical pin. Withdrawal never reclaims a held historical body. Evidence: the holder rows and the body digest. Writes: holder rows through the admitted owner write. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `PlanCollector::withdraw_absent` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:353) → `withdraw_one` → `copy_withdrawn_body`. Shape: a withdrawn member keeps its body holder through `add_withdrawn_member`; only the reconciler (`reconcile.rs`) can reclaim a body, and only when no holder row names it. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/withdrawal.rs::withdrawn_tool_leaves_search_but_keeps_historical_content` (L3) and `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/admin.rs::holder_refcounts_transition_across_unchanged_revised_and_withdrawn_entries` (L3). |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R002.5` | **Orphan grace and fault recovery.** Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` or `AgentComponent.Content` → `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/reconcile.rs::serve`. Rule: A fault before owner commit leaves an orphan. The reconciler reclaims it only after its grace window. An unexpired orphan and a held body survive reconciliation and restart. Evidence: the holder rows and the body digest. Writes: holder rows through the admitted owner write. Depends on: none. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `serve_import` → `reconcile_if_due` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/reconcile.rs:81) → `reconcile_locked`; `ConnectorPack.ReconcileBodies` → `reconcile::serve`. Shape: the grace window is `BlobPolicy::gc_grace_ms` (crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/store/policy.rs:40); the reconciler reads the holder set and reclaims only unheld, expired bodies. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/admin.rs::the_reconciler_releases_an_orphan_body_and_keeps_every_held_one` (L3) covers orphan grace and held bodies. The fault-before-commit restart case is `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/storage_acceptance.rs::orphan_grace_fault` (L3, planned). |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R002.10` | **Receipt verification before model dispatch.** Medulla issues the receipt in R002.9 and verifies it in R002.13. The refusal to dispatch is a harness behaviour owned by MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R076 in MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE. Rule here: a receipt verified against the live head returns `ok`. An invalid, stale, substituted or wrong-scope receipt returns `PROMPT_RECEIPT_INVALID`. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R002.13, MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R076. Shape: the receipt type and the verdict enum are added to `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`; the digest uses `Digest256::sha256`. | Test: `tests/test_prompt_blueprints_served.py::test_receipt_verdicts` (L4) covers valid, stale, substituted and wrong-scope receipts. The zero-dispatch proof is MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R076. |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R008` | **Blob content-addressed store garbage-collection hardening.** Medulla's blob store reseeds cursor identifiers safely. It applies holder-row refcount updates idempotently, so repeated writes never over- or under-count references. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.ReconcileBodies` → `reconcile::serve` and the import-time `reconcile_if_due` → blob `sweep` (crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/store.rs:131) and `put_engine_bodies`. Shape: the policy `BlobPolicy::gc_grace_ms` (`crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/store/policy.rs`) is the grace window; holder rows are set-like so a repeated write is idempotent. | Test: `crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/store/tests.rs::a_referenced_then_released_blob_survives_grace_and_increfs_stay_idempotent_on_count` (L2) and `crates/medulla-server-blob/src/blob/store/tests.rs::gc_reclaims_orphan_keeps_referenced_shared` (L2). |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R009` | **Projection readiness gates reader visibility.** Readers see a pack record only after graph projection completes and visibility is marked. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R011` | **Incompatible Agent Library storage is refused, not upgraded.** When Medulla finds an existing Agent Library store in a format it does not recognize, it refuses to open it with a named AGENT_LIBRARY_FORMAT_UPGRADE_REQUIRED error instead of reading or migrating it, and no version performs an automatic offline upgrade of that layout. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R022` | **Pack index well-formedness validation.** Medulla validates that a `ConnectorPackIndex` declares a supported schema version and a valid connector name. It requires entries sorted by unique URI and only the closed set of entry kinds. Each entry must match its declared URI scheme, and the index holds exactly one server entry. It refuses otherwise with `MALFORMED_INDEX` or `UNKNOWN_ENTRY_KIND`. It also refuses a `schema_version` downgrade and a duplicate entry id, and the error names the field. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `serve_import` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146) → `validate_and_commit` → `prepare` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:95) → `validate_index` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:20) → `validate_header` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:69), `validate_identity` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:170) and `validate_uri_kind` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:450). The schema version check is in `validate_import_identity` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:332): any version other than `CONNECTOR_PACK_SCHEMA_VERSION`, a downgrade included, refuses with `MALFORMED_INDEX`. Shape: violations are `PackViolation` values built by `violation` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:913) whose `detail` names the field; the closed entry kind set is `PackEntryKind` and an unknown kind fails at decode. | Test: `tests/test_connector_pack_planted.py::test_planted_defect_is_refused_by_its_rule` (L4) plants the G1 cases `g1_unsorted`, `g1_scheme`, `g1_two_servers`, `g1_unknown_kind`, `g1_schema_version` (builders in `medulla/testing/synthetic/packs/malformed_index.py`) and asserts the named refusal with unchanged state. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R023` | **Pack size bounds across index, entries, archive and body.** Medulla enforces size bounds on every pack component: index at most 16 MiB, at most 8192 entries, at most 64 references or list items, archive at most 16 MiB, body at most 2 MiB, schema at most 1 MiB, import record at most 4 MiB, and batch size within the kernel budget, refusing any violation with PACK_TOO_LARGE. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `serve_import` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146) → `validate_and_commit` → `prepare` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:95) → `validate_index` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:20) → `validate_section` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:208) and `validate_import_identity`. Shape: the bounds are the constants `MAX_PACK_ENTRIES`, `MAX_PACK_REFERENCES`, `MAX_PACK_IRI_ITEMS`, `MAX_PACK_INDEX_BYTES`, `MAX_PACK_ARCHIVE_BYTES`, `MAX_PACK_BODY_BYTES`, `MAX_PACK_SCHEMA_SECTION_BYTES` and `MAX_PACK_RECORD_BYTES` (crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack.rs:43); list bounds are `BoundedVec` const parameters that fail at decode, the record bound is checked in `PlanCollector::finish` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:470). | Test: `tests/test_connector_pack_planted.py::test_planted_defect_is_refused_by_its_rule` (L4) plants the G2 cases `g2_entries`, `g2_body`, `g2_index`, `g2_references`, `g2_iri_list`, `g2_archive`, `g2_schema` (builders in `medulla/testing/synthetic/packs/malformed_index.py`) and asserts the named refusal with unchanged state. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R024` | **Archive presence, length and digest integrity.** Medulla requires the pack archive to be present and checks its declared length and SHA-256 against the received bytes, refusing with ARCHIVE_MISSING when absent or ARCHIVE_DIGEST_MISMATCH when the hash does not match. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `serve_import` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146) → `validate_and_commit` → `prepare` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:95) → `validate_index` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:20) → `preflight_import` → `read_import_archive` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:439). Shape: `read_import_archive` returns `ARCHIVE_MISSING` for an unknown blob or chunk and `ARCHIVE_DIGEST_MISMATCH` for a wrong length or SHA-256; `rejection_for` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:210) turns either into a `Rejected` result. | Test: `tests/test_connector_pack_planted.py::test_planted_defect_is_refused_by_its_rule` (L4) plants the G3 cases `g3_length`, `g3_sha`, `g3_missing` (builders in `medulla/testing/synthetic/packs/malformed_index.py`) and asserts the named refusal with unchanged state. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R025` | **Archive section layout and offset correctness.** Medulla validates that every archive section's offset and length use checked arithmetic, cover the uncompressed archive exactly once with no overlap, and that the archive itself is stored uncompressed, refusing a malformed layout with MALFORMED_SECTIONS. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `serve_import` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146) → `validate_and_commit` → `prepare` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:95) → `validate_index` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:20) → `validate_section` and `finish_validation` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:508). Shape: `section_bytes` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:895) uses checked arithmetic; `finish_validation` sorts the ranges and refuses a gap or overlap; the archive is stored as received bytes, never compressed. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import.rs::tests::section_ranges_are_checked_without_wrapping` (L2). Planted cases: `tests/test_connector_pack_planted.py::test_planted_defect_is_refused_by_its_rule` (L4) with `g4_overlap`, `g4_gap`, `g4_overflow` in `medulla/testing/synthetic/packs/malformed_index.py`. |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R071.1.2` | **Crosswalk behaviour.** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Code: `validate_crosswalks` (new) in `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/domain_pack/crosswalk.rs`, called by R071.1.3. The fixtures bind Medulla Person, Event and Document to the pinned Schema.org declarations. Each declared relation is `SeeAlso`. Document includes physical documents, so it never becomes `SkosExactMatch` to DigitalDocument. `SkosBroadMatch` means the target is broader than the source. `SkosNarrowMatch` means the target is narrower. Typed authority names each relation explicitly. A reference never implies `owl:sameAs`, class equivalence or a subclass assertion. The mapping preserves continuant, process and temporal-region separation. Fixed bounds: 4096 source classes, 16384 mappings, 2048 bytes per IRI. Depends on: R071.1.1. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `serve_import` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146) → `validate_and_commit` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import.rs:48) → `prepare` (crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:95) → `validate_crosswalks`. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R093` | **Turtle parser blank-node identities do not collide.** Each Turtle parse produces blank-node identities scoped to that parse, so unioning the results of independently parsed graphs introduces no blank-node identity collision. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.reproject` → `serve` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/reproject.rs:26`) → `project_head` (`reproject.rs:74`) → `push_schema_document` (`reproject.rs:261`) → `compose_pack_documents` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema.rs:346`) → `medulla_server_api::schema_compose::scope_blank_nodes` (`crates/medulla-server-api/src/schema_compose.rs:300`). | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R096.1` | **A rejected projection never leaves a head dark without a state.** Caller: the outbox consumer of `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R096`. When projection of a committed head is rejected, the outbox must record the rejection with its stable code. Pack status must report the projection as `failed` with that code and detail, the existing `PackProjectionState::Failed { code, detail }`; no attempt time is added to that state or to the status wire. The consumer retries a retryable rejection with bounded backoff. Retryable codes are the closed list `RETRYABLE_PROJECTION_CODES` in `reproject.rs`: `PACK_PROJECTION_BLOB_UNAVAILABLE` (a body read from the chunk store failed), `PACK_PROJECTION_GRANT_FAILED` (the projection grant could not be written) and `PACK_PROJECTION_STORE_BUSY` (the commit lost a transient race); every other code is not retryable. For a retryable code `settle` records the failed state and leaves the lease unresolved, so the kernel redelivers the row when the lease expires (`LEASE_MS` 120000 ms), which is the backoff; the kernel's bounded retry (`MAX_DELIVERY_ATTEMPTS` 16, `crates/medulla-transaction/src/outbox/rows.rs:66`) then dead-letters it as `exhausted` and the failed state stays. For any other code `settle` rejects the row at once as it does today. A rejection that is not retryable must stay visible until the served reproject method or the next import succeeds. A dark head must never read as applied. Caller chain: outbox consumer `project` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/connector_pack_projection.rs:286`) → `project_head` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/reproject.rs:74`) → `record_projection_failure` (`reproject.rs:193`) → pack status read by `ConnectorPackOp::Status` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:52`). Shape: Edit `reproject.rs` (the failure record with stable code and last-attempt time) and the retryable branch of `settle` in `connector_pack_projection.rs` (`resolve` skips ack and reject for a retryable code); the three codes are produced by `materialize_projection_documents`, the grant step and the commit path; the test file `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/outbox.rs` already exists and gains the test. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/outbox.rs::rejected_projection_is_visible_and_retried` (planned) (L3). |

Re-validate in `apply_pack_source` in `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema.rs` before schema activation. Extend `OntologyInspection` with revision-bound `coverage`. This row creates the domain-to-import crosswalk call site. Writes: the pack head and schema source. Knobs: none. Caller chain: `serve_import` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146`) → `validate_and_commit` (`import.rs:48`) → `prepare` (`import/planning.rs:95`) → `compute_coverage` before `commit_connector_pack` (`import.rs:97`); activation re-validates in `apply_pack_source` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema.rs:293`); read path `handle_ontology_inspect` (`crates/medulla-h-sparql/src/rdf/inspect.rs:42`). | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R099` | **Best-matching prototype by cosine similarity in the numeric kernel.** The in-process numeric extension exposes best_cosine_prototype(query, prototypes), returning nothing when no prototype has a strictly positive cosine with the query and otherwise the index of the first prototype with the maximum positive cosine together with that score. Empty and zero vectors yield no match, a prototype whose length differs from the query is refused with a shape error unless it is all zero, non-finite inputs follow the numeric kernel's existing policy, and the binding enforces the kernel's aggregate input bounds. It is an in-process function, not an engine wire method. Caller chain: Python `numeric.best_cosine_prototype` → pyo3 wrapper `best_cosine_prototype` (`crates/medulla-numeric/src/py.rs:236`, registered at `:558`) → `prototype::best_cosine_prototype` (`crates/medulla-numeric/src/prototype.rs:14`). Shape: Two files: `prototype.rs` (pure kernel: first strictly positive maximum, shape error on a non-zero length mismatch) and `py.rs` (aggregate input bound, `py.detach`). It is an in-process function with no `Method` variant. | Test: `crates/medulla-numeric/src/prototype.rs::tests::first_positive_maximum_and_zero_rows` (L1), `::ragged_zero_row_is_harmless_but_nonzero_mismatch_errors` (L1) and `::empty_and_nonpositive_inputs_have_no_match` (L1); installed-binding `tests/test_numeric_prototype.py::test_matching` (L4), `::test_aggregate_and_container_limits` (L4) and `::test_downstream_label_mapping_and_keyword_call` (L4). |

Verified by the root test `list_after_reingest_returns_new_digest`. |

Rule: include the skill body, the ontology terms it references and the code delta since the last render. The content digest is SHA-256 over the canonical encoding of those three parts in that order. The same graph revision always yields the same digest. Caller: `skill_row` (R100.3). Writes: none. Knobs: none. ## Reviewed mapped definitions

These append-only children preserve the existing parent contracts named below. All remain SPECIFIED; this definition records planned acceptance and does not claim implementation or served receipts.

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R069.100` | **RETIRED: fingerprint identity belongs to repository ingest.** The surviving row MEDULLA-REPO-INGEST-R603.7 owns this requirement. | Retired; see MEDULLA-REPO-INGEST-R603.7. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R100.100` | **Lossless SKILL.md frontmatter and step editing.** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Entry point: new `AgentComponentOp::EditSkillSource { component_id, base_revision, source_digest, patches, operation_id }` in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`. Caller: authenticated dispatch → `handle_agent_component` → `apply_skill_patches` (new, `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/skill_edit.rs`) → `publish_component`. `SkillSourcePatch` is `{range_start, range_end, replacement}` over the original SKILL.md bytes. The request names the source digest, frontmatter field spans and step spans. Step spans use the single step grammar of `MEDULLA-REPO-INGEST-R002.1.1`. It also names the line ending style and unknown-field spans. The function applies bounded byte-range patches and never reparses and reserializes the document. Untouched bytes, order, spacing, comments, line endings and unknown frontmatter fields stay identical. The result validates under R019 (SKILL.md-only import) and R029 (restricted YAML). The response carries the new content digest and source provenance. The SDK owns the write-back edge, through SCION-GOVERNED-WRITEBACK-R004.5. Refusals: `SKILL_PATCH_OVERLAP`, `SKILL_PATCH_ANCHOR_INVALID`, `SKILL_SOURCE_STALE`, `SKILL_SPAN_UNKNOWN`. A refusal writes nothing. Knobs: none. Caller chain: served `AgentComponentOp::EditSkillSource` (new variant in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`, enum at `:1143`) → `handle_agent_component` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/agent.rs:99`) → `apply_skill_patches` (new, `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/skill_edit.rs`) → `publish_component` (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/agent_component/write.rs:39`). Shape: `apply_skill_patches` is a pure byte-range function in a new `medulla-catalog-types` file (no reparse, no reserialize); `SkillSourcePatch` and the new op sit beside it and in `agent_component.rs`; the result is validated by the existing SKILL.md checks (`import/front_matter.rs`, `import/skill_files.rs` in `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/`). Depends on: R100.1, R094.3. | Test: `tests/typed_packs/test_skill_lossless_edit_served.py::test_frontmatter_and_step_edits_preserve_unmodified_bytes` (planned) (L4) and `::test_stale_or_ambiguous_edit_refuses_before_write` (L4). A prompt-blueprint round trip is not accepted evidence. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R100.101` | **Skill consumer compatibility and declared-migration admission.** Subsystem: ontology and packs, with decisions. Entry point: new `AgentComponentOp::AdmitSkillUpgrade`. Caller: arbora invoke certification (ARBORA-FLEET-R034.9) → hand-written Python client → `handle_agent_component` → `admit_skill_upgrade` (new, `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/skill_admission.rs`). `SkillAdmission` binds the prior and new content digests. It also binds the consumer-declared contract revisions, the observed consumer cohort and `verdict`. It carries the migration id, migration digest and `admission_revision`. `verdict` is the closed enum `Compatible`, `BreakingWithMigration`, `BreakingRefused`. Compatibility is judged against consumer-declared contracts. New optional fields are compatible. A removal or type change is breaking. Version equality, contract-pin equality and dependency digests never decide it. A breaking change needs a declared migration bound to the exact old digest, new digest and consumer contract. The migration receipt must pass. Refusals: `SKILL_BREAKING_NO_MIGRATION`, `SKILL_CONSUMER_CONTRACT_CHANGED`, `SKILL_MIGRATION_RECEIPT_INVALID`. Any change of consumer contract, package digest or migration receipt invalidates the admission. Old package versions stay readable. Writes: the admission record. Knobs: none; the rule is Fixed. Caller chain: Python client → served `AgentComponentOp::AdmitSkillUpgrade` (new variant in `crates/medulla-agent-types/src/agent_component.rs`) → `handle_agent_component` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/agent.rs:99`) → `admit_skill_upgrade` (new, `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/skill_admission.rs`) → admission record write in `crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/agent_component/write.rs`. Shape: `SkillAdmission` and the closed `verdict` enum (`Compatible`, `BreakingWithMigration`, `BreakingRefused`) in the new file; a pure function over declared contracts, digests and the migration receipt; register the three refusal codes in `crates/medulla-types/src/contract/server_error_code.rs`. Depends on: R100.1, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805. | Test: `tests/typed_packs/test_skill_compatibility_admission_served.py::test_breaking_upgrade_requires_exact_consumer_migration` (planned) (L4) and `::test_changed_consumer_contract_invalidates_admission` (L4). |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R001.100` | **Versioned nested prompt and tool schemas with incremental compatibility.** Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs` → new `compat_verdict(base: &SchemaDigest, next: &NestedSchema) -> CompatVerdict` in `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/contract_version.rs`. Entries carry `schema_version`, a nested field schema, required status and `compat_revision`. `CompatVerdict` is `Identical`, `AdditiveOptional` or `Breaking`. A new optional field is `AdditiveOptional`. A removed field, a removed required status or a changed type is `Breaking`. The consumer policy of MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R107 admits `AdditiveOptional`. `Breaking` refuses before publication with `PACK_SCHEMA_INCOMPATIBLE`. A stale base digest refuses with `PACK_PLAN_STALE`. The record keeps the base digest, changed paths and verdict. Tool execution authority stays with SCION-CONNECTOR-CONTROL-R003 and ARBORA-FLEET-R002.3 to R002.6. Import dispatches no tool. Caller chain: `serve_import` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146`) → `validate_and_commit` (`import.rs:48`) → `prepare` (`import/planning.rs:95`) → `validate_index` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:20`) → `IndexValidator::validate_schema` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:399`) → `compat_verdict` (new, `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/contract_version.rs`). | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R071.100` | **Canonical hierarchical concept paths.** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Entry point: `ConnectorPack.Import` vocabulary validation. Caller: `validate_and_commit` in `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import.rs` → `validate_concept_paths` (new) in `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/concept_path.rs`. Grammar: segments join with `/`. `%2F` escapes a literal slash and `%25` a literal percent. Segments are NFC-normalized and case-sensitive. A segment has no leading or trailing whitespace. An empty segment refuses. The record carries `schema_version: 1`. The rule resolves the whole path against the pinned vocabulary revision. It checks uniqueness repository-wide and parent and child consistency. Existing published IRIs stay unchanged and are crosswalked, never rewritten. Medulla validation owns canonical identity. Producers submit proposed paths. Refusals: `CONCEPT_PATH_MALFORMED`, `CONCEPT_PATH_DUPLICATE_ALIAS`, `CONCEPT_PATH_PARENT_CONFLICT`, `CONCEPT_PATH_IRI_CONFLICT`, `CONCEPT_PATH_STALE_REVISION`. Each names the conflicting ids. Refusal codes register in `crates/medulla-types/src/contract/server_error_code.rs` (MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R010.100). Writes: the committed pack head only. Caller chain: `serve_import` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146`) → `validate_and_commit` (`import.rs:48`) → `prepare` (`import/planning.rs:95`) → `validate_index` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:20`) → `validate_concept_paths` (new, `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/concept_path.rs`). Shape: New file `import/concept_path.rs` (`mod concept_path;` in `import.rs`) with a pure parser (split on `/`, decode `%2F`/`%25`, NFC, reject empty and padded segments) and a resolver over the pinned vocabulary revision; refusal codes added to `PackViolationCode` in `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/result.rs` and registered in `server_error_code.rs`. Depends on: R071, R071.1.1, MEDULLA-DATAPLAT-VOCAB-R001.1, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R011. | Test: `tests/typed_packs/test_concept_path_admission_served.py::test_duplicate_or_conflicting_paths_refuse_without_iri_changes` (planned) (L4). `test_owner_approved_path_grammar_is_shared` (L4) admits a new path and refuses duplicate, alias, parent and stale revisions. Both read back unchanged published IRIs. |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R101.2` | **Connector contract shapes.** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Code: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/connector_pack-v1.shapes.ttl` holds one node shape per contract class. Caller: `validate_and_commit` validates the contract entry against it. A contract names exactly one connector, one version and one digest. A source object names exactly one ontology class and one key rule. A field mapping names one source field and one predicate of the composed schema. `cp:cursorKind` is one of `opaque`, `timestamp`, `commit`, `offset`, `page_token`. `cp:defaultAcl` is one of `quarantine`, `inherit`, `public`. `cp:privacyClass` is one of `public`, `internal`, `personal`, `secret`. A provenance requirement names at least one required field. The JSON-to-RDF mapping is one pure function, `contract_graph(connector: &str, declaration: &serde_json::Value) -> Result<Graph, PackViolationCode>`, new in `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/contract_rdf.rs` and run by `validate_declaration_body` before the SHACL check. Subject IRIs are `http://knuckles.team/kg/connector-pack/contract/<connector>` for the contract, with `/<object>`, `/<object>/key`, `/<object>/field/<source_field>`, `/cursor`, `/access` and `/provenance` suffixes (each segment percent-encoded). Mapping: `connector` → `cp:contractOf`; `contract.version` → `cp:contractVersion`; `contract.digest` → `cp:contractDigest`; each key of `schema_mappings` → one `cp:SourceObject` linked by `cp:hasSourceObject` with `cp:objectName`; its `ontology_class` → `cp:ontologyClass` (an IRI of the composed schema); its `key_fields[]` → one `cp:KeyRule` linked by `cp:hasKeyRule` with one `cp:keyField` per entry; each `fields` entry `{source_field: predicate}` → one `cp:FieldMapping` linked by `cp:hasFieldMapping` with `cp:sourceField` and `cp:mapsPredicate`; `contract.cursor.kind` → `cp:hasCursorPolicy` and `cp:cursorKind`; `contract.access.{default_acl, acl_field, tenant_field, read_roles[]}` → `cp:hasAccessPolicy` with `cp:defaultAcl`, `cp:aclField`, `cp:tenantField` and one `cp:readRole` per role; `contract.privacy_class` → `cp:privacyClass`; `contract.provenance.required_fields[]` → `cp:requiresProvenance` to one `cp:ProvenanceRequirement` with one `cp:requiredField` per entry. `crates/medulla-core/ontology/connector_pack-v1.ttl` declares the new properties `cp:objectName`, `cp:ontologyClass`, `cp:hasFieldMapping`, `cp:sourceField`, `cp:mapsPredicate`, `cp:keyField` and `cp:requiredField` (datatype properties of xsd:string except `cp:hasFieldMapping`, an object property from `cp:SourceObject` to `cp:FieldMapping`, and `cp:ontologyClass`, an object property to `owl:Class`). A missing required key refuses with `CONTRACT_SHACL_VIOLATION` at the shape, not in the mapping function. Refusal: `CONTRACT_SHACL_VIOLATION`. Writes: none. Knobs: none. Caller chain: `serve_import` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146`) → `validate_and_commit` (`import.rs:48`) → `prepare` (`import/planning.rs:95`) → `validate_index` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:20`) → `IndexValidator::validate_declaration_body` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs:303`) → SHACL check against `connector_pack-v1.shapes.ttl`. Shape: New `contract_rdf.rs` above; edit `crates/medulla-core/ontology/connector_pack-v1.ttl` (the seven properties) and `crates/medulla-core/ontology/connector_pack-v1.shapes.ttl` (one node shape per contract class, `sh:in` lists for `cp:cursorKind`, `cp:defaultAcl`, `cp:privacyClass`); the code reuses `medulla_shacl` as the pack validators already do, and adds `PackViolationCode::ContractShaclViolation` in `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/connector_pack/result.rs`. | Test: `crates/medulla-shacl/tests/connector_pack_shapes.rs::each_contract_shape_accepts_valid_and_refuses_invalid` (planned) (L2). |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R101.9` | **One tenant key for pack heads.** `SourceIngest` must resolve the connector pack under the same tenant key that `ConnectorPack.Import` and `ConnectorPack.Status` use. All three call `persistence::connector_pack::pack_tenant_key`, which returns the verified `tenant_id`. `SourceIngest` never uses the opaque carrier tenant scope of its graph route for a pack lookup. Proving test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/scale.rs::an_imported_pack_maps_source_ingest_records_of_the_same_tenant`. Caller chain: `ConnectorPack.Import` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack.rs:146`) and `ConnectorPack.Status` and served `SourceIngest` (`crates/medulla-h-ingest/src/source_ingestion.rs:404`) → `pack_tenant_key` (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack.rs:28`) → `VerifiedRequestContext::tenant`. Shape: One function `pack_tenant_key` in `crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack.rs` returning the verified tenant id; every pack lookup calls it, never the graph route's `tenant_scope` carrier. | A served test imports a pack whose `connector://resources` declaration holds a `schema_mappings` entry through `ConnectorPack.Import`. It then submits one record under that mapping through `SourceIngest`, which must accept it. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R101.10` | **Mappings come from the connector's declaration.** A connector on Graft SDK 0.4.2 or later serves its data sources and record-type mappings as JSON at `connector://resources`. The pack holds that body as the `resource` entry with that URI. The entry carries a content schema, as every resource entry must. Import decodes its `schema_mappings` (record type to ontology class and field map) with a closed JSON decoder. A body that is not a JSON object, or holds an unknown mapping key, is refused with `MALFORMED_BODY`. Import reads relations from the pack ontology's `owl:ObjectProperty` declarations. The `rdfs:domain` local name is the source resource. The property local name is the relation. The `rdfs:range` local name is the target. A property without one named domain and one named range declares no relation. `SourceIngest` resolves `connector:<connector>#mappings/<type>` against the declared mappings. It resolves `connector:<connector>#relations/<resource>/<relation>` against the ontology relations. The bare `connector:<connector>` form names a declaration with one mapping. The engine accepts no other scheme. The entry kind set has no manifest kind, and the engine reads no YAML. Owner: Medulla. Caller chain: `validate_and_commit` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import.rs:48`) → `prepare` (`import/planning.rs:95`) → closed decoder of the `connector://resources` body (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack/declaration.rs`); then served `SourceIngest` → `resolve_mappings` (`crates/medulla-h-ingest/src/source_ingestion.rs:404`) → `resolve_connector_schema_mapping` (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack.rs:401`) with `mapping_selector` (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack.rs:535`). Shape: `declaration.rs` holds the closed JSON decoder and `schema_mappings`; `PackViolationCode::MalformedBody` is the refusal; relations come from the pack ontology's `owl:ObjectProperty` declarations through `resolve_connector_relationship_mapping`; no second resolver and no manifest kind. | `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/scale.rs::the_selector_resolves_to_the_declared_class_and_field_map`; `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/scale.rs::relations_resolve_from_the_pack_ontology`; `crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack/declaration.rs::refuses_a_malformed_or_open_mapping`. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R101.11` | **Undeclared mappings are refused by type.** `SourceIngest` refuses a mapping reference with `UNKNOWN_MAPPING_REFERENCE` when its scheme is not `connector:`. It refuses the same way when the declaration does not name its record type. A pack with no `connector://resources` entry is refused with `CONNECTOR_SCHEMA_MAPPING_UNAVAILABLE`. A refusal commits no record, cursor or checkpoint. Owner: Medulla. Caller chain: served `SourceIngest` → `resolve_mappings` (`crates/medulla-h-ingest/src/source_ingestion.rs:404`) → `resolve_connector_schema_mapping` (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack.rs:401`) → `mapping_selector` (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack.rs:535`, `UNKNOWN_MAPPING_REFERENCE`) and the missing-declaration branch (`crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack.rs:492`, `CONNECTOR_SCHEMA_MAPPING_UNAVAILABLE`). Shape: Both refusals are returned as the `Err` string of the resolver in `crates/medulla-persistence-records/src/connector_pack.rs`; `crates/medulla-h-ingest/src/source_ingestion.rs` propagates them before any record, cursor or checkpoint is written. | `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/tests/scale.rs::an_undeclared_type_is_refused_with_a_typed_error`. |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105` | **A connector ontology joins the core through subclass edges.** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Entry points: `ConnectorPack.Import` and `ImportOntology` (R103). Rule: every pack class has an `rdfs:subClassOf` path to a core class. A pack never imports the core ontology. A pack that declares `owl:imports <http://knuckles.team/kg/core>` refuses with `ONTOLOGY_PACK_IMPORTS_CORE`. A pack with an orphan class refuses with `ONTOLOGY_ORPHAN` naming each unlinked class. The `standalone` request flag does not exist. The environment switch `MEDULLA_ONTOLOGY_ORPHANS` is deleted, and refuse is the only behaviour. A class query over a core class returns the pack subclasses. Refusal codes register in `crates/medulla-types/src/contract/server_error_code.rs` (MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R010.100). | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105.1` | **Delete the orphan warn switch; the parent-link check refuses.** ConnectorPack import resolves an `ontology-import:` requirement that names the parent catalog `http://knuckles.team/kg` or one of its modules. It reports no unresolved claim for it. A pack ontology may also name the parent catalog in a requirement. The attached ontology keeps its `rdfs:subClassOf` edges in the composed tenant schema. The engine computes each declared class with no subclass path to a parent-catalog class and rejects the import with `ONTOLOGY_ORPHAN`, whose detail is bounded to 1 KiB. The environment switch `MEDULLA_ONTOLOGY_ORPHANS`, the `warn` default and the receipt warning no longer exist; this row is the deletion task that gives `R105` its refuse-only behaviour. Writes: none on refusal. Knobs: none. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105.4` | **The parent catalog holds a service-operations core aligned to the Open Group IT4IT data objects.** The module `service_operations-v1.ttl` is the core source `core:service_operations@1`, and the catalog lists it. Work items: `Issue` and `Ticket` (`sdlc_lifecycle-v1.ttl`) sit under `WorkItem`. `Problem` and `Change` sit under `Ticket`, `ChangeRequest` under `Change`, and the existing `Incident` under `Ticket`. Configuration: `ConfigurationItem` sits under `AssetInstance`, which now sits under `bfo:0000004`. `Application`, `BusinessApplication`, `BusinessService` and `Server` sit under `ConfigurationItem`. `AssignmentGroup` sits under `Organization`. Technology: `TRMStandard` sits under `TechnologyStandard` and `TRMPhase` under `Concept`. Work: `Task` sits under `bfo:0000015` and `Prompt` under `bfo:0000031`. Each edge has a domain and a range. `affects` links Incident, Problem or Change to ConfigurationItem. `touches` links ChangeRequest to ConfigurationItem. `usesTechnology` links ConfigurationItem to TechnologyStandard. `hostedOn` links Application or BusinessService to Server. `relatedTo` is symmetric over Repository, WorkItem and Application. `systemPromptFor` links Prompt to Task. `canHandle` links Skill, Agent or Prompt to Task or Incident, and the adapter vocabulary's `appliesTo` keeps its own domain and range. The datatype property `state` has domain `WorkItem` and range `xsd:string`. `hasPhase` links TechnologyStandard to TRMPhase in the shared namespace, apart from the infrastructure module's own `hasPhase`. The existing `priority` (domain `Requirement`) becomes an `owl:unionOf` of `Requirement` and `WorkItem`, so the shared property stays unintersected. No class or property names a vendor or a product. No IRI has two different parents. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R650` | **Value types, link types and interfaces compile to enforced shapes (rollup).** Subsystem: ontology and packs, object model. Caller: served `ConnectorPack.import` → `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import.rs::run_import` (`:66`) → `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs::validate_ontology_imports` (`:671`) → new `object_model::compile_shapes`. A pack ontology declares `:ValueType`, `:LinkType` and `:Interface` terms. The engine compiles them to SHACL shapes. `crates/medulla-server-api/src/icv_guard.rs::check_before_write` (`:133`) enforces the shapes at every later write. Home: new `crates/medulla-core/src/ontology/object_model.rs`. Object sets, pivots and shortest paths are UQL (`MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R708` stored queries and the graph algorithms in `medulla-compute`). A principal sets one flag per asset: `endorsement`, `warning` or `deprecation`, each with a reason. Ranking uses the boost and penalty knobs of `R108.16`. A warning or deprecation writes one event on the mutation outbox topic `catalog.trust_flag` (`crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/mutation_outbox.rs`). Arbora consumes it and notifies each principal whose stored query (`MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R708.9`, the consumer of trust flags) reads the asset (`ARBORA-OPS-R855.3.1`). A steward marks a term or column critical. A critical element needs an owner, a steward and one active quality rule (`MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R762`). The query returns each critical element that misses one. Assets flagged `owner_missing` (`R106.4`) appear in it. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R803` | **Sensitive data detection proposes classification claims (rollup).** Caller chain: `Method::AnalyticsJob` → `handle_submit_op` (`crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/jobs/submit.rs:114`) → `crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/jobs/dataset_profile.rs` → `medulla_core::sensitive_scan::scan_values`; and `Method::EnrichDocument` → `enrich` (`crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline/web_ingest.rs:31`) → `scan_chunks`. Subsystem: ontology and packs. Entries: the `DatasetProfile` job (MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R761.3) and the served `EnrichDocument` method (MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R651.7). The engine scans columns and document chunks for personal and sensitive data. Each detection above its detector threshold becomes a `ClassificationClaim` of MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R652.1 in state `proposed`. A steward promotes or rejects it through MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R802. Detections are the provider of the tags that `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R706.7` consumes. `MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R761.3` is the caller that passes each column sample to the scan. The scan stores counts and never matched values. Children: `.1` model, `.2` column scan, `.3` job wiring, `.4` refusals, `.5` evidence, `.6` benchmark, `.7` document wiring. Shape: types in the landed `crates/medulla-types/src/sensitive_detect.rs`; scan in new `crates/medulla-core/src/sensitive_scan.rs`; the two callers sit in different crates and share only `medulla-core`. | Root L3 journey: `crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/jobs/sensitive_scan_served_tests.rs::profile_job_proposes_claim_without_values` (L3). |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R803.1` | **Model.** Caller chain: `dataset_profile` executor (R803.3) calls `SensitiveDetector::validate` and `builtin_detectors()` from `crates/medulla-types/src/sensitive_detect.rs`. Subsystem: ontology and packs. Types exist in `crates/medulla-types/src/sensitive_detect.rs` (`SensitiveDetector`, `Detection`, `SensitiveDetectionMethod`). Extend that file. Add `DetectorRule` with variants `Pattern{regex}`, `Checksum{algorithm}`, `Dictionary{terms_digest}`, `Model{exemplar_set}`. `algorithm` is the closed enum `ChecksumAlgorithm{Luhn, IbanMod97}`. Add the field `rule` to `SensitiveDetector`. Add `sample_size` and `scanned_at_ms` to `Detection`. Add `SensitiveDetector::validate` returning `DETECTOR_THRESHOLD_INVALID`, `DETECTOR_RULE_MISMATCH` or `DETECTOR_PATTERN_INVALID`. Add `builtin_detectors()` returning four detectors: `card_number` (Luhn), `email_address`, `iban` (IbanMod97) and `ipv4_address`, each with `knob sensitive.default_threshold`. Knob `sensitive.default_threshold` default 0.8, [0.05, 1.0], ratio. Writes: none. Shape: extend the landed `crates/medulla-types/src/sensitive_detect.rs` (`SensitiveDetector`, `Detection`); `DetectorRule` is a tagged serde enum built like the existing `SensitiveDetectionMethod`. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R652.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-types/tests/sensitive_detect.rs::records_round_trip` (L1) round-trips both records and `validate` rejects each invalid input. Verified by the root slice test `crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/jobs/sensitive_scan_served_tests.rs::profile_job_proposes_claim_without_values`. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R803.2` | **Column scan behavior.** Caller chain: `dataset_profile` executor (R803.3) → `scan_values` → `propose_claims`. Subsystem: ontology and packs. Code: new `crates/medulla-core/src/sensitive_scan.rs` with `scan_values(asset, values, detectors, now_ms) -> Vec<Detection>` and `propose_claims(detections, detectors) -> Vec<ClassificationClaim>`. Rule: the scan reads the first `knob sensitive.max_sample_values` non-null values in profile read order. Default 10000, [100, 1000000], values. `Pattern` runs a compiled regex with size limit `knob sensitive.regex_size_limit` (default 1048576, [65536, 16777216], bytes). `Checksum` applies Luhn or IBAN mod-97 to the digits. `Dictionary` matches case-folded values against the stored term set. `Model` embeds at most `knob sensitive.model_sample_values` values (default 200, [10, 5000], values). It hits when cosine to an exemplar reaches the threshold. `ratio` is hits over sampled values. A claim is proposed when `ratio >= threshold`. The function keeps no value after comparison. Writes: none. Shape: new `crates/medulla-core/src/sensitive_scan.rs` (`pub mod sensitive_scan;` in `crates/medulla-core/src/lib.rs`) with pure functions over `medulla_types::sensitive_detect`; the pattern rule uses `regex::RegexBuilder::size_limit`. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R803.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-core/tests/sensitive_detect.rs::scan_proposes_claim_without_values` (L2) scans card and email columns and asserts claims, counts and that no value appears in any output. Verified by the root slice test. |

The executor loads `builtin_detectors()` plus tenant nodes of type `SensitiveDetector`. It validates every detector before the first scan. Writes: one `ClassificationClaim` node in state `proposed` per passing detector, inside the job mutation batch. Each node links to the catalog column node. No second caller scans values. Shape: new `crates/medulla-h-jobs/src/jobs/dataset_profile.rs` declared in `jobs.rs`, built like `executor_program.rs`; it calls `medulla_core::sensitive_scan::scan_values` once per column and adds the claim nodes to the job mutation batch. The executor must refuse before any scan when a detector fails `validate`. Codes: `DETECTOR_THRESHOLD_INVALID` for a threshold not finite or outside [0, 1]. `DETECTOR_RULE_MISMATCH` for a method that differs from the rule variant. `DETECTOR_PATTERN_INVALID` for a regex that fails to compile or exceeds the size limit. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R804.2` | **Diff and impact behavior.** Caller chain: `handle_gateway` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema.rs`:195) → `DiffOntology` arm → `diff_axioms` and `impact`. Subsystem: ontology and packs. Code: new `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema/ontology_diff.rs` with `diff_axioms(from, to) -> OntologyDiff` and `impact(from, to, snapshot) -> (ChangeImpact, ImpactItems)`. Rule: each side is the set of triples whose subject is an IRI, sorted. `added` and `removed` are subject IRIs present on one side. `changed` holds subject IRIs on both sides with different triple sets. `deprecated` holds IRIs newly marked `owl:deprecated true`. Impact runs on an off-lock graph snapshot with the candidate schema composed in memory. No branch is created. `instances_invalid` counts nodes that fail the new shapes and passed the old. `shapes_broken` counts shapes naming a removed IRI. `mappings_broken` and `queries_broken` count R2RML mappings and stored queries (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R708) whose parsed IRIs include a removed IRI. Shape validation uses the step bound of MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R103.2. Writes: none. Shape: pure functions over `medulla_graph_core::graph::GraphCore` snapshots and `medulla_shacl`; reuse `medulla_server_api::schema_compose::validate_entering_schema`, as `validate` in `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema/approved.rs` does. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R804.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema/ontology_diff_tests.rs::removed_class_reports_impact` (L2) removes one class and asserts all four counts and the item lists. Verified by the root slice test. |

When any count is above zero it opens a change request of kind `ontology.breaking_change` (MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R802.1). The import still commits. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R805.7` | **Data product proposals.** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Caller chain: `Method::AnalyticsJob` (kind `UsageCluster`, MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R764) → usage executor → `data_product::propose_products`. Rule: assets form a cluster when at least `knob data_product.cluster_min_principals` principals (default 3, [2, 50], principals) each queried every asset. The queries fall in the same period. The proposal names the datasets, the glossary terms linked to them and the principal with most queries as owner candidate. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R106.1` | **Model.** Caller chain: the composed schema at bootstrap (`GraphSchemaSources`, `crates/medulla-graph-core/src/graph/schema_sources.rs:200`) loads the module and its shapes, and `check_before_write` (`crates/medulla-server-api/src/icv_guard.rs:133`) enforces them on every `AddTriples`. The core module `metadata_catalog-v1.ttl` holds the catalog vocabulary. Its shapes are `metadata_catalog-v1.shapes.ttl`. The sources are `core:metadata_catalog@1` and `core:metadata_catalog-shapes@1`. The prefix `cat:` is `http://knuckles.team/kg/catalog#`. The prefix `cp:` is `http://knuckles.team/kg/connector-pack#`. Every class rolls up to a Medulla core class and aligns to a W3C class. Assets follow DCAT: `cat:Catalog`, `cat:Asset`, `cat:DataResource`, `cat:Field` and `cat:ToolAsset`. The Agent Library adds `cat:PromptAsset`, `:PromptCandidate`, `cat:SkillAsset` and `cat:AgentAsset`. `cp:Connector` is a `:Tool` and a `dcat:DataService`. Assets link through `cp:ofConnector`, `cp:servesClass`, `cp:servesProperty`, `cat:hasField`, `cp:packVersion` and `cp:digest`. `cat:actionType` names the schema.org Action a tool performs. The core `:providesCapability` is reused. `cp:declaredOwner` and `cat:hasOwner` sit under `:ownedBy`. `cat:hasOwner` and `cat:hasSteward` sit under `prov:wasAttributedTo`. `cat:StewardshipAssignment` is a `prov:Attribution`. It carries `cat:assignedTo`, `cat:principal` and `cat:role`. The roles are `cat:owner`, `cat:steward` and `cat:custodian`, each an `org:Role`. `:AssignmentGroup` is an `org:OrganizationalUnit`. `:derivedFrom` sits under `prov:wasDerivedFrom`, and `cat:lineageTo` is its inverse. `cat:LineageEdge` is a `prov:Derivation` with `cat:from`, `cat:to` and `cat:hop`. `cat:fromSource` links a record to its data resource. `cat:IngestRun` is a `prov:Activity`. The glossary uses `cat:Glossary`, `cat:GlossaryTerm`, `cat:definesClass` and `:definesAsset` over SKOS. Connector-backed assets (`ToolAsset`, `PromptAsset`, `SkillAsset`, `DataResource` and `Field`) must name exactly one connector; `AgentAsset` is an Agent Library asset governed by R108 and has no connector requirement here. The shapes refuse a data resource without one class and a field without one property. They refuse a candidate without a task and an open role, hop or flag value. Shape: edit only `crates/medulla-core/ontology/metadata_catalog-v1.ttl` and `crates/medulla-core/ontology/metadata_catalog-v1.shapes.ttl`; the class tests live in `crates/medulla-rdf/tests/metadata_catalog_classes.rs`. | `crates/medulla-rdf/tests/metadata_catalog_classes.rs::catalog_classes_roll_up_to_eg_core_and_align_to_w3c`, `::owner_steward_and_lineage_properties_specialise_prov`, `::catalog_shapes_accept_a_complete_catalog` and `::catalog_shapes_refuse_incomplete_or_open_vocabulary_records`. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R106.2` | **Behavior.** Subsystem: metadata catalog, shape rules. The batch for one onboarding pass holds one node per tool, prompt, skill, resource and field. Each node has a class under `cat:Asset`. Each node has one `cp:ofConnector`, one `cp:packVersion` and the pack `cp:digest`. A resource or field node has `cp:servesClass` or `cp:servesProperty`. Each node has one `StewardshipAssignment` node (`cat:assignedTo`, `cat:principal`, `cat:role cat:owner`). A pack with no `cp:declaredOwner` gets the importing principal as owner and `cat:flag "owner_missing"`. Each lineage hop is a `cat:LineageEdge` with `cat:from`, `cat:to` and `cat:hop`, plus a direct `cat:lineageTo` edge. `cat:hop` is one of `connector`, `operation`, `resource`, `field`, `ingest`, `transform`. A term binds through `:definesAsset` (`R801.2`). One batch holds at most `knob catalog.onboard_batch_max_triples` triples: default 50000, range [1000, 500000], unit triples. A larger pass splits into 2 or more batches. Arbora removes departed asset nodes with `RemoveTriples` before the add. Add shapes to `metadata_catalog-v1.shapes.ttl` for owner presence and edge pairing. A breaking change is any `ContractBreak`. Digests are `sha256:` plus 64 lowercase hex characters. Both records are serde types with `deny_unknown_fields`; the Rust types are the only schema. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R107.2` | **Behavior.** Subsystem: ontology and packs, import. Caller chain: `run_import` → `contract_diff::diff_release(prior_contract, new_contract) -> Vec<ContractBreak>` in new `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/contract_diff.rs`. It compares the new class, property and field sets with the active version using `OntologyDiff` (`R804`) and the mapping set. No break: the release applies and writes `:supersedes` to the prior version. Any break: the release is stored as pending, a change request opens (`R802.1`), and the active version keeps serving. Virtual-graph queries resolve through the active version only. Removed surfaces leave current queries and stay readable by revision. An expired `StandaloneGrant` flags its ontology `orphan` at the next import. Maximum grant lifetime is 90 days, `Fixed`. Shape: `diff_release` is a pure function over `OntologyDiff` (`crates/medulla-types/src/ontology_diff.rs`) and the mapping set, called by `run_import` (`import.rs:66`); the change request opens through `medulla_core::stewardship::open_change_request`. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R108.16` | **One ranked index across all asset families, callable from queries.** Subsystem: query, catalog search. Caller chain: served `UnifiedQuery` → `handle_unified_query` (`crates/medulla-h-query/src/query/sql_read.rs:309`) → the UDF registered at `crates/medulla-sql/src/sql/exec.rs:873` (`ctx.register_udf`) → new `catalog_relevance`; served SPARQL → `handle_sparql` (`crates/medulla-h-sparql/src/rdf/sparql.rs:32`) → `crates/medulla-rdf/src/sparql/scalar.rs`. Ranked search is a relevance function in the query engine. It is not a served method. SPARQL calls `cat:relevance(?asset, "text")` in `FILTER`, `BIND` or `ORDER BY`. UQL calls `search("text")` in a `MATCH` filter or order. It covers every family of `R106` and `R108`, plus metrics, articles, dashboards, queries and models. It reuses the index of `MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R764` and adds no second index. Score is the sum of name, term-label, concept and description matches. Knobs: `catalog.endorse_boost` default 1.25, range [1.0, 2.0], ratio. `catalog.deprecate_penalty` default 0.5, range [0.1, 1.0], ratio. `catalog.search_page_max` default 100, range [1, 1000], unit hits. The usage prior is capped by `MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R764.2`. `cat:rankComponents` returns the components. The NL planner resolves a business term to assets through it. `CATALOG_QUERY_TOO_LONG` refuses text over 1000 characters. Shape: `catalog_relevance_udf()` beside `epistemic_decay_udf` (`crates/medulla-sql-udfs/src/udfs.rs:141`) and a `cat:relevance` function in `crates/medulla-rdf/src/sparql/scalar.rs`; the usage prior reuses `fuse_with_usage_prior` (`crates/medulla-types/src/asset_usage.rs:44`). Depends on: MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R764.2, R802.7. | `tests/typed_packs/test_catalog_capabilities_served.py::test_one_search_ranks_all_families_with_usage_and_endorsement_boost` (L3). |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R109.4` | **Refusal.** Subsystem: metadata catalog, dashboards. Caller chain: `handle_dashboard_asset` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/catalog/dashboard.rs`, R109.3) → `medulla_core::dashboard::save`. `DASHBOARD_REVISION_CONFLICT`: stale revision; the refusal names the current revision. `DASHBOARD_NOT_GRANTED`: a read without a grant or a write without `edit`. Only the owner changes grants or deletes. `DASHBOARD_BODY_INVALID`: a panel with no query, or a body over `knob catalog.dashboard_body_max_bytes`. `DASHBOARD_PURGED`: a read of a purged asset. All register in `ServerErrorCode`. A refusal never overwrites. Shape: four `ServerErrorCode` variants returned by `dashboard::save` and mapped by `handle_dashboard_asset`; a refused save never writes. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R094.8` | **Record the source-effect receipt on the component revision.** Subsystem: ontology and packs. Depends on: R094.2. | Test: `tests/test_prompt_blueprints_served.py::test_source_effect_receipt_is_recorded_once` (L4). Replay adds no second fact. A cross-tenant caller refuses. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R097.2.7.1` | **External term mapping and coverage model.** Caller chain: `compute_external_coverage` (R097.2.7.2), called by `prepare` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/admin/connector_pack/import/planning.rs:95`), builds and returns these records. Subsystem: ontology and packs. Code: `ExternalTermMapping` and `ExternalOntologyCoverage` in `crates/medulla-types/src/external_schema.rs` (new), each with `schema_version`. Mapping fields: `source_schema_digest`, `source_revision`, `external_iri`, `external_kind`, `platform_iri`, `platform_kind`, `mapping_revision`. Kinds are closed: `Class`, `ObjectProperty`, `DatatypeProperty`, `AnnotationProperty`, `NamedIndividual`. An enumeration is its class plus named individuals. Coverage fields: the digests and revisions above, `pack_revision`, `schema_revision`, `platform_vocabulary_revision`, sorted `terms` and `coverage_digest`. Each term is `{external_iri, kind, result}` where result is `Mapped{platform_iri, provenance_digest}` or `Unmapped{reason: NoDeclaredMapping}`. Knobs: none. Shape: new `crates/medulla-types/src/external_schema.rs` (`pub mod external_schema;` in `crates/medulla-types/src/lib.rs`) with serde structs using `deny_unknown_fields` and one closed enum, built like `OntologyDiff` in `crates/medulla-types/src/ontology_diff.rs`; digests use `medulla_types::contract::Digest256`. Depends on: R097.2.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-types/src/external_schema.rs::tests::mapping_and_coverage_round_trip` (L1). Unknown kinds and duplicate IRIs refuse. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R097.2.7.2` | **External term coverage behaviour.** Caller chain: `prepare` (`import/planning.rs:95`) → `compute_external_coverage`. Subsystem: ontology and packs. Code: `compute_external_coverage` in `crates/medulla-types/src/external_schema.rs`. Only a declared mapping with a kind-compatible platform target is adopted. The original external IRI and kind stay as provenance. Unmapped terms are reported and never added as platform terms. A pack with partial coverage may commit under R097.2 rules. Input order never changes the sorted terms or `coverage_digest` (SHA-256, 64 lowercase hex characters). Knobs: none. Shape: one pure function in `crates/medulla-types/src/external_schema.rs` that sorts terms by `external_iri` before hashing; no I/O. Depends on: R097.2.7.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-types/src/external_schema.rs::tests::reordered_inputs_give_identical_digest` (L2). |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R803.7` | **Document chunk wiring.** Subsystem: ontology and packs, entry `EnrichDocument`. Caller chain: `Method::EnrichDocument` → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline/web_ingest.rs::enrich` (line 31) → chunk loop → `medulla_core::sensitive_scan::scan_chunks`. The function scans at most `knob sensitive.max_chunks` (default 500, [10, 10000], chunks) chunks of one Document. A chunk hits when at least one detector value matches. `Detection.sample_hits` counts hit chunks and `ratio` is hit chunks over scanned chunks. Writes: one `proposed` claim per passing detector, in the same `ChangeEnvelope` as the chunks. Shape: `scan_chunks` sits beside `scan_values` in `crates/medulla-core/src/sensitive_scan.rs`; `enrich` in `web_ingest.rs` calls it per chunk and adds the claims to its `ChangeEnvelope`; the test file `web_ingest_tests.rs` sits beside `web_ingest.rs`. | `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R806` | **Serve schema drift records and repair approval as methods.** Stage: served operations over the drift records of `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R710`. Caller chain: Arbora `ingest.drift.*` → `eg.<service>.SchemaDriftList`, `SchemaDriftGet`, `SchemaRepairPropose` and `SchemaRepairApprove`. Code: new Methods in `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_10.rs`; handlers beside the drift gate of R714. Rule: list and get read the quarantine and report records under the caller's tenant. Propose verifies the repair on a shadow graph and applies nothing. Approve requires a two-person approval receipt bound to the proposal digest, then activates the candidate. Refusals: `DRIFT_NOT_FOUND`, `REPAIR_APPROVAL_MISSING`, `REPAIR_DIGEST_MISMATCH`. Writes: activation through the mutation gateway only on approve. Shape: four `Method` variants in `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_10.rs` beside `ConnectorPack` (line 105); handlers in new `crates/medulla-h-ingest/src/source_ingestion/drift_methods.rs` beside `drift_gate.rs`, routed by `dispatch_catalog_admin_methods` (`crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router/decision_plane.rs:150`); propose uses `RepairProposal::new` (`crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/graph_schema/drift.rs:576`); approve builds `SchemaRepairActivation` (`shadow_branch.rs:38`) and commits through `commit_gateway` (`medulla_server_ops::api::ingest::graph::commit_gateway`, as `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema/approved.rs:41` calls it). Depends on: R710, R714, R715. | Test: `tests/packs/test_schema_drift_methods_served.py::test_propose_then_approve_activates_once` (L4) asserts the quarantine, the preview, each refusal code and one activation. |

| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R808` | **Operator-selected schema import with digest check and readback.** Caller chain: arbora deployment selection → served `GraphSchema` → `handle_gateway` (`crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema.rs:195`) → `AttachApproved` arm → `handle_gateway` of `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema/approved.rs:29`. Stage: served `GraphSchema` `AttachApproved` and `GraphSchemaList`. Arbora deployment selection calls `medulla.graph.GraphSchema` with the chosen candidate. Code: add `expected_digest: Option<String>` (64 lowercase hex characters) to the attach operation in `crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/graph_schema.rs::GraphSchemaOp`. `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema.rs` compares it with `GraphSchemaSource::ontology_sha256` or `shapes_sha256` before it attaches. Rule: an unknown candidate name is refused. A duplicate attach with the same digest is a no-op. An attach with a different digest under the same key is a drift and is refused. `GraphSchemaList` returns the stored digest and the revision, so a client verifies them. Refusals: `SCHEMA_CANDIDATE_UNKNOWN`, `SCHEMA_DIGEST_MISMATCH`, `SCHEMA_SOURCE_DRIFT`. Each changes no schema. Evidence: the list entry names origin, revision and digest after a restart. Writes: the existing attach commit. Shape: one `Option<String>` field on `GraphSchemaOp::AttachApproved` (`crates/medulla-catalog-types/src/graph_schema.rs:103`) and one comparison in `admit` (`approved.rs:48`); `GraphSchemaList` already returns the stored digests. Depends on: none. | Test: `tests/graph_schema_digest_served.rs::mismatch_and_drift_are_refused_and_digest_reads_back` (L3) asserts each code and the digest after a restart. |
| `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R809` | **Served canonical hash of a Turtle payload.** Stage: pack import and ontology inspect, served `OntologyCanonicalHash`. Caller chain: served `OntologyCanonicalHash` → `crates/medulla-h-sparql/src/rdf/dispatch.rs` (beside `OntologyInspect`, line 315) → handler `crates/medulla-h-sparql/src/rdf/ontology_hash.rs` → `canonical_turtle_hash` (`crates/medulla-rdf/src/ontology_hash.rs`). The method canonicalizes the payload with `RDFC-1.0` and returns the SHA-256 digest (64 lowercase hex characters). The hash is stable under prefix changes, statement order and blank-node relabelling. Refusal: `TURTLE_INVALID`. Writes: none. Consumer: pack import (`ConnectorPack.Import`) compares hashes. Shape: new `Method::OntologyCanonicalHash` beside `OntologyInspect` in `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_07.rs`; `canonical_turtle_hash` parses with `oxttl` (feature `rdf`), canonicalizes per RDFC-1.0 and hashes with `sha2`; the handler is built like `handle_ontology_inspect` (`crates/medulla-h-sparql/src/rdf/inspect.rs:42`) and routed from `crates/medulla-h-sparql/src/rdf/dispatch.rs:315`. Depends on: none. | Test: `tests/ontology/test_canonical_hash_served.py::test_reordered_serializations_hash_equal` (L4). It asserts equal hashes for two reordered serializations and `TURTLE_INVALID` for bad input. |
